{"forms":[{"form":"air masses","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"airmass","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["a widespread body of air, the properties of which can be identified as: (a) having been established while that air was situated over a particular region of the Earth's surface (airmass source region) and (b) undergoing specific modifications while in transit away from the source region.
